actions = ('collection', 'collection')

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('actions', [('clear', 'clock'), ('collection', 'collection'),
                                         ('collection', 'clear'), ('collection', 'clock')])
    def test_ownership_serializes_independent_workers_and_clock_clear(qualified_site, actions):
        from threading import Barrier, Thread
        from ipmi_monitor.sel_management import SELUnavailable
        (_client, app, _db, _models, runtime, commands), _state, _registry = qualified_site
        gate, results = Barrier(2), []
        commands.clear()
    
        def worker(action):
            with app.app_context():
                target = runtime.sel_manager.target(BMC)
                gate.wait(timeout=5)
                try:
                    if action == 'collection':
                        ticket = runtime.sel_manager.begin_collection(BMC)
                        results.append(('collection', ticket['attempt_id']))
                    else:
                        operation = runtime.sel_manager.claim(target, action, 'independent-worker')
                        results.append(operation.state)
                except SELUnavailable as exc:
                    results.append(exc.code)
                except Exception as exc:
                    results.append('unexpected:' + type(exc).__name__)
    
        workers = [Thread(target=worker, args=(action,)) for action in actions]
        for worker in workers:
            worker.start()
        for worker in workers:
            worker.join(timeout=10)
        assert all(not worker.is_alive() for worker in workers)
        if actions == ('clear', 'clock'):
            assert sorted(results) == ['operation_busy_or_cooldown', 'queued']
            with app.app_context():
                assert runtime.SELOperation.query.count() == 1
        else:
            winners = [value for value in results if isinstance(value, tuple) or value == 'queued']
            losers = [value for value in results if value in ('operation_busy_or_cooldown', 'collection_busy_or_maintenance')]
>           assert len(winners) == len(losers) == 1, results

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

self = <http.server.HTTPServer object at 0x10da12360>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('tree,kind,role,prefix', [
        (tree, kind, role, prefix) for tree in ('src/ipmi_monitor/templates', 'templates')
        for role in ('admin', 'readonly') for kind, prefix in (('dashboard',''), ('server',''), ('server','/fleet/ipmi'))])
    def test_full_rendered_sel_pages_keep_machine_names_text_and_local_health_urls(
            sel_site, login_as, tmp_path, tree, kind, role, prefix):
        from html import unescape
        from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
        from threading import Thread
        from pathlib import Path
        import json
        import os
        import re
        import shutil
        from jinja2 import FileSystemLoader
        from tests.test_dashboard_ai_reports import CHROMIUM
    
        client, app, db, models, _runtime, _commands = sel_site
        login_as(client, role)
        hostile = '<img src=x onerror=window.SEL_XSS=1>machine'
        with app.app_context():
            models['Server'].query.filter_by(bmc_ip=BMC).update({'server_name': hostile})
            models['ServerStatus'].query.filter_by(bmc_ip=BMC).update({'server_name': hostile})
            db.session.commit()
        headers = {'X-Forwarded-Prefix': prefix, 'X-Fleet-Authenticated': 'true',
                   'X-Fleet-Auth-User': 'testadmin' if role == 'admin' else 'testreader',
                   'X-Fleet-Auth-Role': role} if prefix else {}
        paths = ['/api/servers', f'/api/server/{BMC}/sel/health', f'/api/server/{BMC}/events']
        responses = {prefix + path: client.get(path, headers=headers).get_json() for path in paths}
        previous = app.jinja_loader
        try:
            app.jinja_loader = FileSystemLoader(str(Path.cwd()/tree))
            app.jinja_env.cache.clear()
            route = '/' if kind == 'dashboard' else f'/server/{BMC}'
            page = client.get(route, headers=headers).get_data(as_text=True)
        finally:
            app.jinja_loader = previous
            app.jinja_env.cache.clear()
        # Entire actual rendered page and startup run. Only unrelated HTTP responses
        # are empty fixture data; the tested API inputs are actual application output.
        probe = '''<script>setTimeout(() => {
            try {
                if (typeof populateDeviceSelectors === 'function') {
                    serversData = FIXTURE_SERVERS; populateDeviceSelectors();
                }
                const panel=document.getElementById('selHealthPanel');
                const result={xss:window.SEL_XSS===1,unsafeOptions:document.querySelectorAll('option img,option svg,option [onerror]').length,
                    optionNames:[...document.querySelectorAll('#summaryDevices option,#tasksDevices option,#rcaDevice option')].map(option=>option.textContent),
                    panelText:panel?.textContent || '',buttons:panel?.querySelectorAll('button').length || 0};
                const node=document.createElement('pre');node.id='sel-full-page-result';node.textContent=JSON.stringify(result);document.body.append(node);
            } catch(error) { const node=document.createElement('pre');node.id='sel-full-page-result';node.textContent=JSON.stringify({fatal:String(error)});document.body.append(node); }
        },800);</script>'''.replace('FIXTURE_SERVERS', json.dumps(responses[prefix+'/api/servers']).replace('</', '<\\/'))
        page = page.replace('</body>', probe+'</body>')
        requests = []
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_GET(self):
                requests.append(self.path)
                content = page.encode() if self.path == prefix + route else json.dumps(responses.get(self.path, {})).encode()
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8' if self.path == prefix + route else 'application/json')
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(content)
    
            def log_message(self, *_):
                pass
    
        executable = os.environ.get('IPMI_AICSS_CHROMIUM') or shutil.which('chromium') or shutil.which('chromium-browser') or str(CHROMIUM)
        assert Path(executable).is_file(), 'Real Chromium required for complete-page SEL regression'

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <http.server.HTTPServer object at 0x10e96ba70>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

tmp_path = PosixPath('/private/var/folders/2f/ntb_0p9558v4wfcwr5_64dg00000gn/T/pytest-of-hanneszietsman/pytest-10629/test_validated_producer_comple0')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x10e932040>
fault = 'none'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('fault', ['none', 'reaped_receipt_busy', 'final_identity_and_receipt_busy', 'physical_wait'])
    def test_validated_producer_completion_survives_only_reaped_receipt_loss(
            collection_site, child_tool, tmp_path, monkeypatch, fault):
        """Actual PTY producer + real SQLite writer at only the post-wait receipt."""
        app, db, models, runtime, alerts = collection_site
        make, children = child_tool
        adapter = make()
        executable = tmp_path / 'ipmitool'
        # Extend the existing real-child fixture with only fixed read-only identity replies.
        source = executable.read_text().replace('def response(values):', '''device_reads = 0
    def response(values):
        global device_reads
        if values[:2] == [6,1]:
            device_reads += 1
            device = bytes.fromhex(DEVICE_REPLY)
            return bytes([device[0] ^ 1]) + device[1:] if IDENTITY_FAULT and device_reads > 1 else device
        if values[:2] == [6,55]: return bytes.fromhex(GUID_REPLY)'''
            .replace('DEVICE_REPLY', repr(DEVICE.hex())).replace('GUID_REPLY', repr(GUID.hex()))
            .replace('IDENTITY_FAULT', repr(fault == 'final_identity_and_receipt_busy')))
        executable.write_text(source)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.sel_manager, 'adapter', lambda target: adapter)
        snapshots, fingerprints, receipts = [], [], []
        original_snapshot, original_fingerprint = adapter.snapshot, adapter.fingerprint
        def observe_snapshot(reservation):
            result = original_snapshot(reservation)
            snapshots.append(deepcopy(result))
            return result
        def observe_fingerprint():
            result = original_fingerprint()
            fingerprints.append(result)
            return result
        monkeypatch.setattr(adapter, 'snapshot', observe_snapshot)
        monkeypatch.setattr(adapter, 'fingerprint', observe_fingerprint)
        original_child = runtime.sel_manager.collection_child
        def receive_child(ticket, state, child=None):
            if fault == 'physical_wait' and state == 'running':
                original_wait = child.wait
                def physical_wait(*args, **kwargs):
                    original_wait(*args, **kwargs)
                    # Same code as a receipt failure must not imply the same provenance.
                    raise sel.SELUnavailable('collection_child_identity_unavailable')
                monkeypatch.setattr(child, 'wait', physical_wait)
            if state != 'reaped' or fault not in {'reaped_receipt_busy', 'final_identity_and_receipt_busy'}:
                return original_child(ticket, state, child)
            blocker = db.engine.connect()
            try:
                blocker.exec_driver_sql('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                try:
                    return original_child(ticket, state, child)
                except sel.SELUnavailable as exc:
                    receipts.append(exc.code)
                    raise
            finally:
                blocker.rollback(); blocker.close()  # Writer is gone before real save/common finish.
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.sel_manager, 'collection_child', receive_child)
        with app.app_context():
            events = runtime.collect_ipmi_sel(BMC, 'fixture-host')
>           assert len(children) == 1 and children[0].poll() is not None
E           assert (0 == 1)
E            +  where 0 = len([])

tests/test_sel_collection_state.py:220: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <ipmi_monitor.sel_management.register_sel_management.<locals>.Manager object at 0x10cf774d0>
bmc_ip = '192.0.2.40'

    def begin_collection(self, bmc_ip):
        target = self.target(bmc_ip)
        target_id = target.id
        state = self.collection_state(target)
        if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()
>           raise SELUnavailable('collection_store_unavailable') from None
E           ipmi_monitor.sel_management.SELUnavailable: collection_store_unavailable

            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()

            db.session.rollback()

tests/test_sel_collection_state.py:333: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <ipmi_monitor.sel_management.register_sel_management.<locals>.Manager object at 0x10cf774d0>
bmc_ip = '192.0.2.40'

    def begin_collection(self, bmc_ip):
        target = self.target(bmc_ip)
        target_id = target.id
        state = self.collection_state(target)
        if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('producer', ['worker', 'bulk'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', [None, 'acquisition', 'storage'])
    def test_normal_producers_use_atomic_native_success_and_baseline_boundary(
            collection_site, native_boundary, monkeypatch, producer, failure):
        from queue import Queue
        from threading import Event

    def _boot_identity():
        if sys.platform.startswith('linux'):
            return Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip()
        if sys.platform == 'darwin':
            value, length = _Timeval(), ctypes.c_size_t(ctypes.sizeof(_Timeval))
            libc = ctypes.CDLL('/usr/lib/libSystem.B.dylib', use_errno=True)
            if libc.sysctlbyname(b'kern.boottime', ctypes.byref(value), ctypes.byref(length), None, 0):

tests/test_sel_collection_state.py:469: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <ipmi_monitor.sel_management.register_sel_management.<locals>.Manager object at 0x10cf774d0>
bmc_ip = '192.0.2.40'

    def begin_collection(self, bmc_ip):
        target = self.target(bmc_ip)
        target_id = target.id
        state = self.collection_state(target)
        if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()
>           raise SELUnavailable('collection_store_unavailable') from None
E           ipmi_monitor.sel_management.SELUnavailable: collection_store_unavailable

tests/test_sel_collection_state.py:530: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <ipmi_monitor.sel_management.register_sel_management.<locals>.Manager object at 0x10cf774d0>
bmc_ip = '192.0.2.40'

    def begin_collection(self, bmc_ip):
        target = self.target(bmc_ip)
        target_id = target.id
        state = self.collection_state(target)
        if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()

tests/test_sel_collection_state.py:692: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <ipmi_monitor.sel_management.register_sel_management.<locals>.Manager object at 0x10cf774d0>
bmc_ip = '192.0.2.40'

    def begin_collection(self, bmc_ip):
        target = self.target(bmc_ip)
        target_id = target.id
        state = self.collection_state(target)
        if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()
>           raise SELUnavailable('collection_store_unavailable') from None
E           ipmi_monitor.sel_management.SELUnavailable: collection_store_unavailable

            # The exact live producer may retry its own lost failure receipt.
            # This is completion through finish/CAS, never age-only recovery.
            ticket = state.get('active_ticket') or {}
            try:
                owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
                own_terminal_read = (
                    not target.active_operation and
                    ticket.get('process_instance') == PROCESS_INSTANCE and
                    all(ticket.get('owner', {}).get(key) == owner.get(key)
                        for key in ('pid', 'start', 'boot', 'namespace')) and
                    isinstance(ticket.get('child'), dict) and
                    ticket.get('child_state') in {'running', 'reaped'} and
                    (utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(ticket['started_at'].rstrip('Z'))).total_seconds()
                        > READ_SECONDS + REAP_SECONDS and
                    time.monotonic() > ticket['deadline'] + REAP_SECONDS and
                    child_is_terminal(ticket))
            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                own_terminal_read = False
            if own_terminal_read:
                self.finish_collection(ticket, error='device_deadline_exceeded')
                target = self.target(bmc_ip)
                state = self.collection_state(target)
            # Recovery inspects actual local owner/child identities outside a write lock.
            if state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                try:
                    self.recover_collection(bmc_ip, state['active_attempt_id'])
                except SELUnavailable:
                    db.session.rollback()
                    raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance') from None
        try:
            target = self.collection_lock(target_id)
            server = self.current_server(bmc_ip)
            state = self.collection_state(target)
            if self.binding(server) != target.binding or target.active_operation or state.get('active_attempt_id'):
                raise SELUnavailable('collection_busy_or_maintenance')
            owner = process_identity(os.getpid())
            ticket = {'attempt_id': str(uuid.uuid4()), 'target_id': target.id,
                      'bmc_ip': bmc_ip, 'binding': target.binding, 'revision': target.revision,
                      'fingerprint': target.fingerprint, 'generation': target.generation,
                      'snapshot_epoch': target.snapshot_epoch, 'started_at': stamp(utcnow()),
                      'owner': owner, 'process_instance': PROCESS_INSTANCE,
                      'child': None, 'child_state': 'none',
                      'deadline': time.monotonic() + READ_SECONDS}
            state.update(active_attempt_id=ticket['attempt_id'], active_ticket=ticket,
                         last_attempt_at=ticket['started_at'], outcome='in_progress', safe_reason=None)
            target.collection_state = json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True)
            db.session.commit()
            return json.loads(json.dumps(ticket))
        except SELUnavailable:
            db.session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()
>           raise SELUnavailable('collection_store_unavailable') from None

concurrent = 'lease'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('concurrent', ['lease', 'success'])
    def test_observe_does_not_overwrite_concurrent_collection_commit(collection_site, monkeypatch, concurrent):
        app, db, _models, runtime, _alerts = collection_site
        manager = runtime.sel_manager
        committed = []
        info = {'entries': 7, 'free_bytes': 128, 'percent_used': 0,
                'last_add': '02000000', 'last_erase': '00000000'}
        with app.app_context():
            target = manager.target(BMC)
            target.health = json.dumps({'info': info})
            fingerprint = json.loads(target.fingerprint)
            original_generation = target.generation
            db.session.commit()
        def during_info():
            with app.app_context():
                ticket = manager.begin_collection(BMC)
                if concurrent == 'success':
                    assert manager.finish_collection(ticket, snapshot=snapshot())['outcome'] == 'persisted'
                committed.append(json.loads(manager.target(BMC).collection_state))
            # An older probe cannot apply this erase after a newer collection commit.
            return dict(info, last_erase='01000000')
        adapter = SimpleNamespace(fingerprint=lambda: fingerprint, info=during_info, logging=lambda: 8,
            clock=lambda: {'native_seconds': 0, 'offset_minutes': None, 'quality': 'uninitialized'}, close=lambda: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(manager, 'adapter', lambda target: adapter)
        with app.app_context():
            manager.observe(BMC)
            target = manager.target(BMC)

s = None, cls = None, object_hook = None, parse_float = None, parse_int = None
parse_constant = None, object_pairs_hook = None, kw = {}

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,
            parse_int=None, parse_constant=None, object_pairs_hook=None, **kw):
        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance
        containing a JSON document) to a Python object.
    
        ``object_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).
    
        ``object_pairs_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with
        the result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.
        The return value of ``object_pairs_hook`` will be used instead of the
        ``dict``.  This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If
        ``object_hook`` is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes
        priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",
                                      s, 0)
        else:
            if not isinstance(s, (bytes, bytearray)):
>               raise TypeError(f'the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, '
                                f'not {s.__class__.__name__}')
E               TypeError: the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, not NoneType

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('result', ['complete', 'empty', 'acquisition_failure', 'storage_failure'])
    def test_actual_single_host_manual_route_finishes_native_batch(collection_site, native_boundary, monkeypatch, result):
        from sqlalchemy import event
        from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
        app, db, models, runtime, alerts = collection_site
        adapter, _calls = native_boundary()
        normal_raw = adapter.raw
        if result == 'empty':
            def empty_raw(netfn, command, *data):
                if (netfn, command) == (10, 0x40): return bytes.fromhex('5100008000020000000000000002')
                return normal_raw(netfn, command, *data)
            monkeypatch.setattr(adapter, 'raw', empty_raw)
        if result == 'acquisition_failure':
            monkeypatch.setattr(adapter, 'raw', lambda *args: (_ for _ in ()).throw(sel.SELUnavailable('device_timeout')))
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.sel_manager, 'adapter', lambda target: adapter)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.sel_manager, 'tick', lambda ip: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'build_sensor_cache', lambda ip: None)
        scheduled = []
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.threading, 'Thread', lambda target, **kw: SimpleNamespace(start=lambda: scheduled.append(target)))
        client = app.test_client()
        with client.session_transaction() as session:
            session.update(logged_in=True, username='testadmin', user_role='admin')
        response = client.post('/api/collect?bmc_ip=' + BMC)
        assert response.status_code == 200 and len(scheduled) == 1
        def refuse_insert(*args): raise OperationalError('fixture insert refused', {}, Exception('synthetic'))
        if result == 'storage_failure': event.listen(models['IPMIEvent'], 'before_insert', refuse_insert)
        try:
            scheduled[0]()  # Real handler/collector/persistence; only scheduling is controlled.
        finally:
            if result == 'storage_failure': event.remove(models['IPMIEvent'], 'before_insert', refuse_insert)
        with app.app_context():

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,

        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance

        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).

        the result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.

        ``dict``.  This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If
        ``object_hook`` is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes
        priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",

collection_site = (<Flask 'ipmi_monitor.app'>, <SQLAlchemy>, {'AlertHistory': <class 'ipmi_monitor.app.AlertHistory'>, 'AlertRule': <cla...r.app' from '/Users/hanneszietsman/CrypotAI/ipmi-monitor-ccc90-cipher3-20261111/tests/../src/ipmi_monitor/app.py'>, [])
redfish_reader = <function redfish_reader.<locals>.make at 0x10e872fb0>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x10bfb42f0>
producer = 'worker', result = 'complete'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('producer', ['worker', 'bulk', 'manual'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('result', ['complete', 'empty', 'failure', 'storage_failure'])
    def test_explicit_redfish_mode_preserves_all_producers_without_native_claims(
            collection_site, redfish_reader, monkeypatch, producer, result):
        from queue import Queue
        from threading import Event
        from sqlalchemy import event
        from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
        app, db, models, runtime, alerts = collection_site
        page = {'Members': [] if result == 'empty' else [{'Id': 'rf-1', 'EntryType': 'SEL',
            'Created': '2026-11-11T01:00:00Z', 'Message': 'Power fault', 'Severity': 'Warning'}]}
        if result == 'failure': page['Members@odata.nextLink'] = '//foreign.invalid/Entries'
        client, calls = redfish_reader(page)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'get_redfish_client', lambda ip: client)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.subprocess, 'run', lambda *a, **kw: pytest.fail('native child forbidden'))
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.subprocess, 'Popen', lambda *a, **kw: pytest.fail('native child forbidden'))
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'build_sensor_cache', lambda *a: pytest.fail('native cache forbidden'))
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'check_ssh_xid_errors', lambda *a: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'get_servers', lambda: {BMC: 'fixture-host'})
        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'get_collection_workers', lambda: 1)
        with app.app_context():
            models['Server'].query.filter_by(bmc_ip=BMC).update({'protocol': 'redfish'}); db.session.commit()
        def refuse(*a): raise OperationalError('fixture refused', {}, Exception('synthetic'))
        if result == 'storage_failure': event.listen(models['IPMIEvent'], 'before_insert', refuse)
        try:
            if producer == 'bulk': runtime.collect_all_events()
            elif producer == 'worker':
                jobs = Queue(); jobs.put(('sel', BMC, 'fixture-host')); jobs.put(None)
                monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_collection_queue', jobs); monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_shutdown_event', Event())
                runtime.collection_worker(994)
            else:
                monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.threading, 'Thread', lambda target, **kw: SimpleNamespace(start=target))
                http = app.test_client()
                with http.session_transaction() as session: session.update(logged_in=True,username='testadmin',user_role='admin')
                assert http.post('/api/collect?bmc_ip='+BMC).status_code == 200
        finally:
            if result == 'storage_failure': event.remove(models['IPMIEvent'], 'before_insert', refuse)
        with app.app_context():
            manager = runtime.sel_manager; target = manager.target(BMC)
>           state = json.loads(target.collection_state)

s = None, cls = None, object_hook = None, parse_float = None, parse_int = None
parse_constant = None, object_pairs_hook = None, kw = {}

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,
            parse_int=None, parse_constant=None, object_pairs_hook=None, **kw):
        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance
        containing a JSON document) to a Python object.
    
        ``object_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).
    
        ``object_pairs_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with
        the result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.
        The return value of ``object_pairs_hook`` will be used instead of the
        ``dict``.  This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If
        ``object_hook`` is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes
        priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",
                                      s, 0)
        else:
            if not isinstance(s, (bytes, bytearray)):
>               raise TypeError(f'the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, '
                                f'not {s.__class__.__name__}')
E               TypeError: the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, not NoneType

    def test_explicit_redfish_mode_preserves_all_producers_without_native_claims(
            collection_site, redfish_reader, monkeypatch, producer, result):
        from queue import Queue
        from threading import Event
        from sqlalchemy import event
        from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
        app, db, models, runtime, alerts = collection_site
        page = {'Members': [] if result == 'empty' else [{'Id': 'rf-1', 'EntryType': 'SEL',
            'Created': '2026-11-11T01:00:00Z', 'Message': 'Power fault', 'Severity': 'Warning'}]}
        if result == 'failure': page['Members@odata.nextLink'] = '//foreign.invalid/Entries'
        client, calls = redfish_reader(page)

        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.subprocess, 'run', lambda *a, **kw: pytest.fail('native child forbidden'))

        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'get_collection_workers', lambda: 1)
        with app.app_context():
            models['Server'].query.filter_by(bmc_ip=BMC).update({'protocol': 'redfish'}); db.session.commit()
        def refuse(*a): raise OperationalError('fixture refused', {}, Exception('synthetic'))
        if result == 'storage_failure': event.listen(models['IPMIEvent'], 'before_insert', refuse)
        try:
            if producer == 'bulk': runtime.collect_all_events()
            elif producer == 'worker':
                jobs = Queue(); jobs.put(('sel', BMC, 'fixture-host')); jobs.put(None)
                monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_collection_queue', jobs); monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_shutdown_event', Event())
                runtime.collection_worker(994)

        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'get_collection_workers', lambda: 1)

                jobs = Queue(); jobs.put(('sel', BMC, 'fixture-host')); jobs.put(None)
                monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_collection_queue', jobs); monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, '_shutdown_event', Event())
                runtime.collection_worker(994)

                monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.threading, 'Thread', lambda target, **kw: SimpleNamespace(start=target))
                http = app.test_client()

                assert http.post('/api/collect?bmc_ip='+BMC).status_code == 200

        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).

        for JSON integers (e.g. float).

        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.

        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.

s = None, cls = None, object_hook = None, parse_float = None, parse_int = None
parse_constant = None, object_pairs_hook = None, kw = {}

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,
            parse_int=None, parse_constant=None, object_pairs_hook=None, **kw):
        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance
        containing a JSON document) to a Python object.
    
        ``object_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).
    
        ``object_pairs_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with
        the result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.
        The return value of ``object_pairs_hook`` will be used instead of the
        ``dict``.  This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If
        ``object_hook`` is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes
        priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",
                                      s, 0)
        else:
            if not isinstance(s, (bytes, bytearray)):
>               raise TypeError(f'the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, '
                                f'not {s.__class__.__name__}')
E               TypeError: the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, not NoneType
